title:	LLM-classified category field on Task, Question, and Decision entities
state:	OPEN
author:	marcus-sa

## Problem

Tasks, questions, and decisions have no domain classification. Agents can't filter by area, the Management Agent can't report by domain, and the governance feed can't group items by what part of the business they affect.

## Goal

Add a `category` field to Task, Question, and Decision entities. LLM-classified during extraction, never manually assigned at creation.

---

## Schema

```sql
DEFINE FIELD category ON TABLE task TYPE option<string>
  ASSERT $value IN ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] OR $value IS NONE;

DEFINE FIELD category ON TABLE question TYPE option<string>
  ASSERT $value IN ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] OR $value IS NONE;

DEFINE FIELD category ON TABLE decision TYPE option<string>
  ASSERT $value IN ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] OR $value IS NONE;
```

## Category Definitions (Shared Constant)

One source of truth used by extraction prompt and all agent prompts:

| Category | What it means | Examples |
|----------|--------------|----------|
| `engineering` | Building, implementing, fixing, deploying code | Task: "Implement JWT auth middleware", Decision: "Use PostgreSQL over MongoDB" |
| `research` | Investigating, evaluating, comparing, exploring unknowns | Task: "Research pricing models for B2B SaaS", Decision: "Defer database choice pending benchmark results" |
| `marketing` | Outreach, content, positioning, launch activities | Task: "Write launch announcement blog post", Decision: "Position as developer-first tool" |
| `operations` | Setup, configuration, process, infrastructure admin | Task: "Set up staging environment", Decision: "Use Terraform for infra-as-code" |
| `design` | Wireframes, prototypes, UX, visual design, user flows | Task: "Design onboarding flow wireframes", Decision: "Single-page onboarding over multi-step wizard" |
| `sales` | Prospect outreach, demos, pitching, qualifying leads | Task: "Identify 10 target users for early feedback", Decision: "Target mid-market B2B first" |

Classify based on the **area the entity affects**, not where it came up. "We decided to use JWT" is `engineering` even if discussed during a design session.

## Extraction Prompt Update

Add `category` to Task and Decision extraction schemas:

```typescript
// Task
{
  kind: "task",
  text: string,
  confidence: number,
  evidence: string,
  assignee_name?: string,
  category?: "engineering" | "research" | "marketing" | "operations" | "design" | "sales"
}

// Decision
{
  kind: "decision",
  text: string,
  confidence: number,
  evidence: string,
  category?: "engineering" | "research" | "marketing" | "operations" | "design" | "sales"
}
```

## CategoryBadge Component

Reusable component for rendering category across chat, graph, and feed:

```typescript
const categoryIcons: Record<TaskCategory, string> = {
  engineering: "⚙️",
  research: "🔍",
  marketing: "📢",
  operations: "🔧",
  design: "🎨",
  sales: "🤝",
};
```

Small pill with icon + category name, styled with entity-type-appropriate muted color.

## Management Agent Category Awareness

When reporting status, group by category across all entity types:

```
Project Auth System:
- Engineering: 2 decisions (Use JWT, PostgreSQL over MongoDB), 3 tasks active
- Research: 1 open question (session fallback strategy?), 1 task active
- Design: 1 decision (single-page onboarding), 1 task completed (wireframes)
```

Support category-based queries:
- "what engineering decisions have we made?" → filters decisions by category
- "what research is outstanding?" → filters tasks + questions by category
- "show me everything related to marketing" → cross-entity category filter

## Eval Cases

Task classification:
- "We need to research pricing models" → `category: "research"`
- "Let's build the auth middleware next" → `category: "engineering"`
- "I should reach out to potential customers" → `category: "sales"`
- "Need to set up CI/CD pipeline" → `category: "operations"`
- "Design the dashboard wireframes" → `category: "design"`
- "Write a launch blog post" → `category: "marketing"`
- "Research the best way to implement caching" → `category: "research"` (investigation, not building)

Decision classification:
- "We'll use PostgreSQL for the main database" → `category: "engineering"`
- "Let's target mid-market B2B companies first" → `category: "sales"`
- "We'll go with a single-page onboarding flow" → `category: "design"`
- "Let's use Terraform for infrastructure" → `category: "operations"`
- "We'll position as a developer-first platform" → `category: "marketing"`

## Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] `category` field on Task, Question, and Decision entities in SurrealDB schema
- [ ] Category definitions as shared constant (single source of truth)
- [ ] Extraction prompt classifies category on Task and Decision entities
- [ ] CategoryBadge component reusable across chat, graph, feed
- [ ] Management Agent groups/filters tasks, questions, and decisions by category
- [ ] Eval cases for category classification (tasks + decisions)
- [ ] Categories are LLM-classified, never user-assigned at creation

## Related
- #38 — Observation entity (also uses same category enum)
- #30 — Governance feed (groups items by category)

## Split from
#37

## Entity Extraction Pipeline - Complete Analysis

I've traced the entire extraction pipeline in the palembang codebase. Here's a comprehensive overview:

## 1. ENTITY TYPE DEFINITIONS

    id: string;
    kind: EntityKind;
    text: string;
    confidence: number;

  };
  ```

**Extraction-Specific Types:**

- **Key Types:**
  - `GraphEntityRecord` (line 13): RecordId with table discrimination

  - `PersistableExtractableEntityKind` (line 10): Excludes "workspace" and "person"

**Extraction Prompt:**
- **Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/extraction/prompt.ts`
- **Function:** `buildExtractionSystemPrompt(input: { onboarding: boolean }): string` (line 1)
- **Key Rules:**
  - Lines 5: Entity kinds = project, feature, task, decision, question
  - Line 6: Never emit kind: person

  - Lines 57-60: Placeholder rejection (rejects "my project", "the feature", "it", etc.)
  - Lines 62-65: Document text behavior guidance

**Zod Schema for LLM Output:**
- **Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/extraction/schema.ts`
- **Lines 3-29:** Entity validation schema
  - Required fields: `tempId`, `kind`, `text`, `confidence`, `evidence`
  - Optional fields: `assignee_name`, `resolvedFromMessageId`
  - Entity kind enum (line 5): `"project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question"`
  - Text constraints: min 3, max 200 characters

- **Lines 31-38:** Relationship schema
- **Lines 40-44:** Output schema (entities array, relationships array, tools array)
- **Lines 50-62:** `parseExtractionOutput()` validates JSON response

## 3. LLM CALL & RESPONSE PARSING

**Extraction Generation:**
- **Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/extraction/extract-graph.ts`
- **Function:** `extractStructuredGraph()` (lines 10-77)
- **LLM Call:** Line 32 - `generateObject()` from `ai` package
  - Model: `input.extractionModel` (configurable)
  - Schema: `extractionResultSchema` (Zod validation)
  - System prompt: Built via `buildExtractionSystemPrompt()`
  - **Multi-part prompt construction (lines 36-57):**
    1. Conversation history (reference resolution)
    2. Current message metadata
    3. Resolved-from lineage constraints
    4. Existing graph context (semantic index of prior extracted entities)
    5. Workspace scope (workspace name + existing project names)
    6. Optional section heading
    7. Current source text
  
- **Response Parsing:** Line 60 - Automatic via Zod schema validation
- **Context Formatters (lines 79-146):**
  - `formatExtractionConversationHistory()`: Previous messages with IDs
  - `formatExtractionCurrentMessage()`: Current message with metadata
  - `formatExtractionGraphContext()`: Prior extracted entities with confidence
  - `formatWorkspaceScope()`: Workspace name + project names
  - `formatResolvedFromConstraints()`: Allowed message IDs for resolution

## 4. ENTITY PERSISTENCE LAYER

**Main Persistence Function:**
- **Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/extraction/persist-extraction.ts`
- **Function:** `persistExtractionOutput()` (lines 22-216)
  
**Flow:**
1. **Post-validation (lines 49-65):**
   - `postValidateEntities()` filters entities by:
     - Minimum text length (3 chars)
     - Confidence threshold (default 0.6)
     - Evidence grounding in source text
   - `postValidateRelationships()` filters by confidence

2. **Entity Upsert (lines 76-152):**
   - Calls `upsertGraphEntity()` for each entity
   - Returns record ID and whether entity was created

3. **Provenance Edges (lines 47-58, 96-107, 129-140):**
   - Links extracted entities to source message/document
   - Creates `extraction_relation` edges with:
     - Confidence, extraction timestamp
     - From/to text snippets
     - Evidence and resolved-from message references

4. **Entity Relations (lines 154-185):**
   - Creates `entity_relation` edges between extracted entities
   - Stores relationship kind, confidence, from/to text

5. **Assignee Resolution (lines 137-152):**
   - Looks up person by name in workspace
   - Updates entity with owner/assignee references

**Entity Upsert Logic:**
- **Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/extraction/entity-upsert.ts`
- **Function:** `upsertGraphEntity()` (lines 17-185)

**Deduplication Strategy (lines 33-115):**
1. Exact name match check (normalized)
2. Embedding similarity search (cosine distance)
3. Fuzzy name match + high similarity (>0.95) → merge
4. Medium similarity (0.8-0.95) → link with POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE relation
5. No match → create new entity

**Entity Creation (lines 117-184):**
- **Function:** `buildEntityRecordContent()` (lines 254-318)
- **Project/Feature fields (lines 263-279):**
  - `name`, `status` ("active"), `embedding`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
- **Task fields (lines 282-292):**
  - `title`, `status` ("open"), `extraction_confidence`, `extracted_at`, `source_message`, `embedding`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
- **Decision fields (lines 295-305):**
  - `summary`, `status` ("extracted"), `extraction_confidence`, `extracted_at`, `source_message`, `embedding`, `created_at`, `updated_at`
- **Question fields (lines 308-317):**
  - `text`, `status` ("open"), `extraction_confidence`, `extracted_at`, `source_message`, `embedding`, `created_at`, `updated_at`

**Graph Edge Creation:**
- Lines 142-155: Project/Feature hierarchy via `has_project` and `has_feature` edges
- Lines 157-164: Task/Decision/Question belong_to project via `belongs_to` edges
- Lines 166-177: Possible duplicate detection via `entity_relation` edges

---

## 5. SURREAL SCHEMA DEFINITIONS

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/schema/surreal-schema.surql`

### Task Table (lines 79-94)
```sql
DEFINE TABLE task SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD title ON task TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD owner ON task TYPE option<record<person>>;
DEFINE FIELD owner_name ON task TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD deadline ON task TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD status ON task TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD priority ON task TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON task TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD updated_at ON task TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD source_message ON task TYPE option<record<message>>;
DEFINE FIELD extraction_confidence ON task TYPE option<float>;
DEFINE FIELD extracted_at ON task TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON task TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE INDEX task_status_deadline ON task FIELDS status, deadline;
DEFINE INDEX task_source_message ON task FIELDS source_message;
DEFINE INDEX idx_task_embedding ON task FIELDS embedding HNSW DIMENSION 1536 DIST COSINE;
```

### Decision Table (lines 96-117)
```sql
DEFINE TABLE decision SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD summary ON decision TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD rationale ON decision TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD decided_by ON decision TYPE option<record<person>>;
DEFINE FIELD decided_by_name ON decision TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD decided_at ON decision TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD status ON decision TYPE string ASSERT $value IN ["extracted", "proposed", "confirmed", "superseded", "provisional", "inferred", "contested"];
DEFINE FIELD inferred_by ON decision TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD confirmed_by ON decision TYPE option<record<person>>;
DEFINE FIELD confirmed_at ON decision TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD options_considered ON decision TYPE option<array<string>>;
DEFINE FIELD based_on ON decision TYPE option<array<record>>;
DEFINE FIELD confirmation_notes ON decision TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON decision TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD updated_at ON decision TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD source_message ON decision TYPE option<record<message>>;
DEFINE FIELD extraction_confidence ON decision TYPE option<float>;
DEFINE FIELD extracted_at ON decision TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON decision TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE INDEX decision_status_decided_at ON decision FIELDS status, decided_at;
DEFINE INDEX decision_source_message ON decision FIELDS source_message;
DEFINE INDEX idx_decision_embedding ON decision FIELDS embedding HNSW DIMENSION 1536 DIST COSINE;
```

### Question Table (lines 119-133)
```sql
DEFINE TABLE question SCHEMAFULL;
DEFINE FIELD text ON question TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD assigned_to ON question TYPE option<record<person>>;
DEFINE FIELD assigned_to_name ON question TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD status ON question TYPE string;
DEFINE FIELD context ON question TYPE option<string>;
DEFINE FIELD created_at ON question TYPE datetime;
DEFINE FIELD updated_at ON question TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD source_message ON question TYPE option<record<message>>;
DEFINE FIELD extraction_confidence ON question TYPE option<float>;
DEFINE FIELD extracted_at ON question TYPE option<datetime>;
DEFINE FIELD embedding ON question TYPE option<array<float>>;
DEFINE INDEX question_status ON question FIELDS status;
DEFINE INDEX question_source_message ON question FIELDS source_message;
DEFINE INDEX idx_question_embedding ON question FIELDS embedding HNSW DIMENSION 1536 DIST COSINE;
```

### Key Relationship Tables:
- **extraction_relation** (lines 222-235): Links messages/chunks to extracted entities with confidence and provenance
- **entity_relation** (lines 237-250): Links entities to each other with relationship kind
- **belongs_to** (lines 256-257): Links tasks/decisions/questions to projects/features
- **has_project** (lines 205-206): Links workspaces to projects
- **has_feature** (lines 202-203): Links projects to features

---

## 6. CHAT PROCESSOR INTEGRATION

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/chat/chat-processor.ts`

**Message Processing Flow (lines 19-299):**
1. Load extraction conversation context (lines 51-55)
   - Gets last 30 messages, excluding current
   - Used for reference resolution (pronouns, etc.)
2. Load graph context (line 56)
   - Gets last 60 extracted entities with confidence
3. Extract from attachment if present (lines 66-108)
4. Extract from user text (lines 110-119)
   - Calls `extractStructuredGraph()` with conversation + graph context
5. Persist extraction (lines 121-145)
   - Calls `persistExtractionOutput()` with extraction result
6. Emit extraction events to client (lines 286-290)
7. Generate assistant response (lines 173-231)
8. Persist embeddings (lines 276-283)

---

## 7. KEY VALIDATION & THRESHOLDS

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/extraction/validation.ts`

**Confidence Thresholds:**
- `shouldStoreExtraction()` (line 3): Default 0.6 (configurable)
- `shouldDisplayExtraction()` (line 7): Default 0.85
- `hasGroundedEvidence()` (line 11): Evidence must appear verbatim in source text

**Post-Validation Filters (lines 20-68):**
- Duplicate tempIds → keep highest confidence
- Text length < 3 → reject
- Evidence not in source → reject
- Confidence below threshold → reject

---

## 8. CONTEXT LOADING

**Location:** `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/extraction/context-loaders.ts`

- **`loadExtractionConversationContext()`** (lines 30-56): Loads up to 31 messages, excludes current, returns last 30
- **`loadConversationGraphContext()`** (lines 58-114): Queries extraction_relation edges, deduplicates by entity ID, limits to 60 results
- **`readEntityText()`** (entity-text.ts): Maps entity table to text field:
  - Project/Feature/Workspace: `name`
  - Task: `title`
  - Decision: `summary`
  - Question: `text`

---

## Summary Path from Extraction to Persistence

```
User Message
    ↓
extractStructuredGraph() [extract-graph.ts line 32]
    ↓ (uses prompt.ts + schema.ts)
generateObject() [ai package]
    ↓ (LLM returns JSON)
parseExtractionOutput() [schema.ts line 50]
    ↓ (validates against Zod schema)
persistExtractionOutput() [persist-extraction.ts line 22]
    ↓ (post-validates)
postValidateEntities() + postValidateRelationships() [validation.ts]
    ↓ (for each entity)
upsertGraphEntity() [entity-upsert.ts line 17]
    ↓ (dedup: exact match → fuzzy → similarity → create new)
loadWorkspaceKindCandidates() [entity-upsert.ts line 320]
    ↓ (creates or merges)
CREATE [table] CONTENT {...} [Surreal SDK]
    ↓
createProvenanceEdge() [entity-upsert.ts line 230]
    ↓ (links to source message/chunk)
RELATE message:id → extraction_relation → entity:id
    ↓
Results emitted to client via SSE events
```

This pipeline ensures comprehensive entity extraction with deduplication, confidence filtering, semantic matching, and full provenance tracking.

#### **EntityDetailPanel** 
File: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/client/components/graph/EntityDetailPanel.tsx`

Sections:
1. Header with badge + title (line 142)
2. Metadata section (lines 148-182)
   - Status
   - Confidence
   - Created date
   - Owner name
   - Priority
3. Rationale section (lines 184-189)
4. Relationships section (line 191, uses `RelationshipList`)
5. Provenance section (line 193)
6. Actions (lines 195-213)
   - Confirm Decision (if status in `["extracted", "proposed", "provisional", "inferred"]`)
   - Override Decision
   - Mark Complete (for tasks)

#### **RelationshipList** 
File: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/client/components/graph/RelationshipList.tsx`

Groups relationships by kind + direction, displays with EntityBadge for each related entity

#### **ProvenanceSection**
File: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/client/components/graph/ProvenanceSection.tsx`

Shows:
- Source kind (Message or Document chunk)
- Confidence score
- Extracted date
- Evidence quote
- Jump to message link (for messages)

### 5. API TYPES & DATA FLOW

#### **Extraction Event** (`contracts.ts`, lines 129-134)
```typescript
export type ExtractionEvent = {
  type: "extraction";
  messageId: string;
  entities: ExtractedEntity[];
  relationships: ExtractedRelationship[];
};
```

#### **ExtractedEntity** (`contracts.ts`, lines 36-43)
```typescript
export type ExtractedEntity = {
  id: string;
  kind: EntityKind;
  text: string;
  confidence: number;
  sourceKind: SourceKind;
  sourceId: string;
};
```

#### **EntityDetailResponse** (`contracts.ts`, lines 206-231)
```typescript
export type EntityDetailResponse = {
  entity: {
    id: string;
    kind: EntityKind;
    name: string;
    data: Record<string, unknown>;  // Flexible for status, priority, etc.
  };
  relationships: Array<{
    id: string;
    kind: EntityKind;
    name: string;
    relationKind: string;
    direction: "incoming" | "outgoing";
    confidence: number;
  }>;
  provenance: Array<{
    sourceId: string;
    sourceKind: "message" | "document_chunk";
    confidence: number;
    extractedAt: string;
    evidence?: string;
    evidenceSource?: string;
    resolvedFrom?: string;
    fromText?: string;
  }>;
};
```

#### **GraphResponse** (`contracts.ts`, lines 201-204)
```typescript
export type GraphResponse = {
  nodes: ReagraphNode[];
  edges: ReagraphEdge[];
};
```

#### **ReagraphNode** (`contracts.ts`, lines 179-188)
```typescript
export type ReagraphNode = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  fill: string;  // Hex color from transform
  data: {
    kind: EntityKind;
    connectionCount: number;
    status?: string;  // Optional, displayed in graph
  };
};
```

---

### 6. API ENDPOINTS

#### **Entity Detail Route**
File: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/entities/entity-detail-route.ts`

Endpoint: `GET /api/entities/{entityId}?workspaceId={workspaceId}`
- Uses `getEntityDetail()` from graph/queries.ts
- Returns complete EntityDetailResponse

#### **Entity Search Route**
File: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/entities/entity-search-route.ts`

Endpoint: `GET /api/entities/search?workspaceId={id}&q={query}&limit={limit}`
- Supports optional `projectId` parameter for scoped search
- Returns array of `SearchEntityResponse`

#### **Graph Route**
File: `/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/server/graph/graph-route.ts`

Endpoint: `GET /api/graph/{workspaceId}?[center={entityId}&depth={1-3}|project={projectId}]`
- Uses `transformToReagraph()` to convert DB records to graph visualization format
- Returns GraphResponse

---

### 7. STATE MANAGEMENT

**View State** (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/app/src/client/stores/view-state.ts`):

Zustand store with:
- `selectedEntityId` — currently selected entity in graph
- `graphViewMode` — "project" or "focused"
- `graphProjectId` — filter by project
- `graphCenterId` — center node for focused view
- `graphDepth` — 1-3, traversal depth
- `highlightMessageId` — message to highlight when jumping from graph

---

### 8. COMPONENT USAGE PATTERNS

#### **In Chat (EntityCard)**
- Shows kind as uppercase label (styled with `.entity-kind`)
- Shows status as uppercase label (styled with `.entity-status`)
- Compact, card-based layout
- Confidence displayed as "Confidence 0.95"

#### **In Graph Detail Panel**
- EntityBadge inline with title (`.entity-badge-inline`)
- Metadata section displays entity properties flexibly
- Status and confidence extracted from `entity.data` object

#### **In Knowledge Graph**
- Nodes colored by `entityColor(kind)` hex values
- Node size based on `connectionCount`
- Status available in node.data but not rendered in graph canvas

---

### 9. KEY FILE LOCATIONS & IMPORTS

| Purpose | File | Key Exports |
|---------|------|------------|
| Type definitions | `/app/src/shared/contracts.ts` | EntityKind, ExtractedEntity, EntityDetailResponse |
| Entity extraction schema | `/app/src/server/extraction/schema.ts` | extractionEntitySchema, ExtractionPromptOutput |
| Chat component defs | `/app/src/shared/chat-component-definitions.ts` | entityCardPropsSchema, extractionSummaryPropsSchema |
| Badge component | `/app/src/client/components/graph/EntityBadge.tsx` | EntityBadge |
| Detail panel | `/app/src/client/components/graph/EntityDetailPanel.tsx` | EntityDetailPanel |
| Graph transform | `/app/src/server/graph/transform.ts` | transformToReagraph, entityColor |
| Theme helpers | `/app/src/client/components/graph/graph-theme.ts` | entityColor, entityMutedColor, edgeStyle |
| Styles | `/app/src/client/styles.css` | --entity-{kind}, .entity-badge-inline, .entity-detail-* |
| View state | `/app/src/client/stores/view-state.ts` | useViewState hook |

---

### 10. DESIGN TOKENS (DESIGN_TOKENS.md)

**Visual Language Principles:**
- Scandinavian clarity, typography-led, content-first
- Entity colors are the *only* non-neutral colors in the product
- Never use raw Tailwind colors — always use entity tokens
- Muted backgrounds + accent left borders for cards
- Graph nodes use accent fill at 80% opacity

**Color Palette:**
- 6 entity types with distinct hues
- Foreground variants for text on colored backgrounds
- Muted variants for subtle indicators and backgrounds

---

This codebase uses a **cohesive design system** where:
1. Entity types are mapped to semantic colors via CSS variables
2. Colors appear consistently in chat cards, graph nodes, badges, and detail panels
3. All entity metadata (status, confidence, etc.) flows through `entity.data` as flexible key-value pairs
4. Reusable components (EntityBadge, EntityCard) ensure consistency across views
5. Server-side transform resolves CSS variables to hex for WebGL rendering

     1→import { z } from "zod";
     2→
     3→const extractionEntityBaseSchema = z.object({
     4→  tempId: z.string().min(1),
     5→  kind: z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"]),
     6→  text: z.string().min(3).max(200),
     7→  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1),
     8→  evidence: z.string().min(1),
     9→}).strict();
    10→
    11→const extractionEntityWithAssigneeSchema = extractionEntityBaseSchema.extend({
    12→  assignee_name: z.string().min(1),
    13→}).strict();
    14→
    15→const extractionEntityWithResolvedFromSchema = extractionEntityBaseSchema.extend({
    16→  resolvedFromMessageId: z.string().min(1),
    17→}).strict();
    18→
    19→const extractionEntityWithAssigneeAndResolvedFromSchema = extractionEntityBaseSchema.extend({
    20→  assignee_name: z.string().min(1),
    21→  resolvedFromMessageId: z.string().min(1),
    22→}).strict();
    23→
    24→const extractionEntitySchema = z.union([
    25→  extractionEntityBaseSchema,
    26→  extractionEntityWithAssigneeSchema,
    27→  extractionEntityWithResolvedFromSchema,
    28→  extractionEntityWithAssigneeAndResolvedFromSchema,
    29→]);
    30→
    31→export const extractionRelationshipSchema = z.object({
    32→  kind: z.string().min(1),
    33→  fromTempId: z.string().min(1),
    34→  toTempId: z.string().min(1),
    35→  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    36→  fromText: z.string().min(1),
    37→  toText: z.string().min(1),
    38→}).strict();
    39→
    40→export const extractionResultSchema = z.object({
    41→  entities: z.array(extractionEntitySchema),
    42→  relationships: z.array(extractionRelationshipSchema),
    43→  tools: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    44→}).strict();
    45→
    46→export type ExtractionPromptEntity = z.infer<typeof extractionEntitySchema>;
    47→export type ExtractionPromptRelationship = z.infer<typeof extractionRelationshipSchema>;
    48→export type ExtractionPromptOutput = z.infer<typeof extractionResultSchema>;
    49→
    50→export function parseExtractionOutput(payload: unknown):
    51→  | { ok: true; data: ExtractionPromptOutput }
    52→  | { ok: false; error: string } {
    53→  const result = extractionResultSchema.safeParse(payload);
    54→  if (result.success) {
    55→    return { ok: true, data: result.data };
    56→  }
    57→
    58→  return {
    59→    ok: false,
    60→    error: result.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Invalid extraction payload",
    61→  };
    62→}
    63→

     5→import { Factuality } from "autoevals";

     7→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     8→import { entityPrecisionScorer } from "./scorers/entity-precision";
     9→import { entityRecallScorer } from "./scorers/entity-recall";
    10→import { noPhantomPersonsScorer } from "./scorers/no-phantom-persons";

    20→import { normalizeForSubstring } from "./scorers/shared";
    21→import { extractStructuredGraph } from "../app/src/server/extraction/extract-graph";
    22→import { persistExtractionOutput, appendExtractedTools } from "../app/src/server/extraction/persist-extraction";
    23→import { loadExtractionConversationContext, loadConversationGraphContext } from "../app/src/server/extraction/context-loaders";
    24→import type { SourceRecord } from "../app/src/server/extraction/types";
    25→import { loadWorkspaceProjects } from "../app/src/server/workspace/workspace-scope";
    26→import {
    27→  type EvalRuntime,
    28→  setupEvalRuntime,
    29→  teardownEvalRuntime,
    30→  seedWorkspace,
    31→  seedConversationContext,
    32→  seedUserMessage,
    33→  seedGraphEntities,
    34→  loadWorkspacePeopleCount,
    35→} from "./eval-test-kit";
    36→
    37→const extractionModel = process.env.EXTRACTION_MODEL ?? "anthropic/claude-3.5-haiku";
    38→const autoevalModel = requireEnv("AUTOEVAL_MODEL");
    39→
    40→const cacheDir = process.env.EVAL_CACHE_DIR ?? "eval-results/cache";
    41→const cachePath = join(cacheDir, "extraction-cache.json");
    42→const resultCache = loadCache(cachePath);
    43→const cases = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(process.cwd(), "evals", "data", "golden-cases.json"), "utf8")) as GoldenCase[];
    44→assertAutoevalEnv();
    45→
    46→// strict_single is reserved for unambiguous one-entity probes.
    47→// Cases where multiple extractions are semantically valid should use multi_allowed.
    48→const intentScoreWeights: Record<
    49→  GoldenCaseIntent,
    50→  Record<
    51→    | "entity-precision"
    52→    | "entity-recall"
    53→    | "no-extra-entities"
    54→    | "no-phantom-persons"
    55→    | "evidence-grounded"
    56→    | "no-context-bleed"
    57→    | "evidence-source-current-message"
    58→    | "resolved-from-lineage"
    59→    | "tool-filtering"
    60→    | "forbidden-kinds"
    61→    | "factuality"
    62→    | "relation-recall",
    63→    number
    64→  >
    65→> = {
    66→  strict_single: {
    67→    "entity-precision": 0.18,
    68→    "entity-recall": 0.18,
    69→    "no-extra-entities": 0.2,
    70→    "no-phantom-persons": 0.13,
    71→    "evidence-grounded": 0.09,
    72→    "no-context-bleed": 0.05,
    73→    "evidence-source-current-message": 0.06,
    74→    "resolved-from-lineage": 0.04,
    75→    "tool-filtering": 0.08,
    76→    "forbidden-kinds": 0.1,
    77→    factuality: 0.02,
    78→    "relation-recall": 0,
    79→  },
    80→  multi_allowed: {
    81→    "entity-precision": 0.23,
    82→    "entity-recall": 0.23,
    83→    "no-extra-entities": 0.05,
    84→    "no-phantom-persons": 0.12,
    85→    "evidence-grounded": 0.09,
    86→    "no-context-bleed": 0.05,
    87→    "evidence-source-current-message": 0.07,
    88→    "resolved-from-lineage": 0.04,
    89→    "tool-filtering": 0.04,
    90→    "forbidden-kinds": 0.06,
    91→    factuality: 0.03,
    92→    "relation-recall": 0.04,
    93→  },
    94→};
    95→
    96→let runtime: EvalRuntime;
    97→
    98→beforeAll(async () => {
    99→  runtime = await setupEvalRuntime("extraction");
   100→}, 120_000);
   101→
   102→afterAll(async () => {
   103→  await teardownEvalRuntime(runtime);
   104→}, 120_000);
   105→
   106→const factualityScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({
   107→  name: "factuality",
   108→  description: "Average factual grounding score for extracted entity texts against matched provenance evidence snippets.",
   109→  scorer: async ({ input, output }) => {
   110→    if (output.extractedEntities.length === 0) {
   111→      return { score: 1 };
   112→    }
   113→
   114→    let total = 0;
   115→    for (const entity of output.extractedEntities) {
   116→      const snippet = resolveEntityEvidenceSnippet(entity.text, output.evidenceRows, input.input);
   117→      const result = await Factuality({
   118→        input: snippet,
   119→        output: entity.text,
   120→        expected: snippet,
   121→        model: autoevalModel,
   122→      });
   123→      total += result.score ?? 0;
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    return { score: total / output.extractedEntities.length };
   127→  },
   128→});
   129→
   130→evalite<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>("Extraction Golden Cases", {
   131→  data: cases.map((testCase) => ({ input: testCase, expected: testCase })),
   132→  task: async (input) => runCase(input),
   133→  scorers: [
   134→    entityPrecisionScorer,
   135→    entityRecallScorer,
   136→    noExtraEntitiesScorer,
   137→    noPhantomPersonsScorer,
   138→    evidenceGroundedScorer,
   139→    noContextBleedScorer,
   140→    evidenceSourceCurrentMessageScorer,
   141→    resolvedFromLineageScorer,
   142→    toolFilteringScorer,
   143→    forbiddenKindsScorer,
   144→    relationRecallScorer,
   145→    factualityScorer,
   146→  ],
   147→  columns: ({ input, output, scores }) => [
   148→    { label: "Model", value: extractionModel },
   149→    { label: "Precision", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "entity-precision")) },
   150→    { label: "Recall", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "entity-recall")) },
   151→    { label: "NoExtra", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "no-extra-entities")) },
   152→    { label: "NoPeople", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "no-phantom-persons")) },
   153→    { label: "Evidence", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "evidence-grounded")) },
   154→    { label: "NoCtxBleed", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "no-context-bleed")) },
   155→    { label: "EvSrc", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "evidence-source-current-message")) },
   156→    { label: "Lineage", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "resolved-from-lineage")) },
   157→    { label: "Tools", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "tool-filtering")) },
   158→    { label: "Kinds", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "forbidden-kinds")) },
   159→    { label: "RelRecall", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "relation-recall")) },
   160→    { label: "Factual", value: formatScoreCell(scoreByName(scores, "factuality")) },
   161→    { label: "Intent", value: input.intent },
   162→    { label: "Case", value: input.id },
   163→    { label: "Expected", value: input.expectedEntities.length },
   164→    { label: "Extracted", value: output.extractedEntities.length },
   165→    { label: "People", value: `${output.personCount}/${output.ownerPersonCount}` },
   166→    {
   167→      label: "Avg",
   168→      value: computeWeightedAverage(input.intent, scores).toFixed(2),
   169→    },
   170→  ],
   171→});
   172→
   173→function scoreByName(
   174→  scores: Array<{ name: string; score: number | null }>,
   175→  name: string,
   176→): number {
   177→  const match = scores.find((score) => score.name === name);
   178→  if (!match || match.score === null || Number.isNaN(match.score)) {
   179→    throw new Error(`Missing score for scorer: ${name}`);
   180→  }
   181→
   182→  return match.score;
   183→}
   184→
   185→function formatScoreCell(value: number): string {
   186→  return value.toFixed(2);
   187→}
   188→
   189→function computeWeightedAverage(
   190→  intent: GoldenCaseIntent,
   191→  scores: Array<{ name: string; score: number | null }>,
   192→): number {
   193→  const weights = intentScoreWeights[intent];
   194→  if (!weights) {
   195→    throw new Error(`Unknown golden case intent: ${intent}`);
   196→  }
   197→
   198→  let weightedTotal = 0;
   199→  let weightTotal = 0;
   200→  for (const [scorerName, weight] of Object.entries(weights)) {
   201→    if (weight <= 0) {
   202→      continue;
   203→    }
   204→
   205→    weightedTotal += scoreByName(scores, scorerName) * weight;
   206→    weightTotal += weight;
   207→  }
   208→
   209→  if (weightTotal === 0) {
   210→    throw new Error(`No scorer weights configured for intent: ${intent}`);
   211→  }
   212→
   213→  return weightedTotal / weightTotal;
   214→}
   215→
   216→function resolveEntityEvidenceSnippet(
   217→  entityText: string,
   218→  evidenceRows: Array<{ evidence?: string; fromText?: string; model?: string }>,
   219→  fallbackInput: string,
   220→): string {
   221→  const normalizedEntityText = normalizeForSubstring(entityText);
   222→  const matched = evidenceRows.find((row) => normalizeForSubstring(row.fromText ?? "") === normalizedEntityText);
   223→  const snippet = matched?.evidence ?? evidenceRows[0]?.evidence ?? fallbackInput;
   224→  return snippet.trim().length > 0 ? snippet : fallbackInput;
   225→}
   226→
   227→async function runCase(testCase: GoldenCase): Promise<ExtractionEvalOutput> {
   228→  const cacheKey = buildCaseCacheKey(extractionModel, testCase);
   229→  const cached = resultCache[cacheKey];
   230→  if (cached) {
   231→    return {
   232→      ...cached,
   233→      extractedTools: cached.extractedTools ?? [],
   234→      extractedRelations: cached.extractedRelations ?? [],
   235→    };
   236→  }
   237→
   238→  const { workspaceRecord, workspaceName, projectRecord, conversationRecord, ownerPersonCount } = await seedWorkspace(runtime.surreal, testCase.workspace_name);
   239→  const conversationId = conversationRecord.id as string;
   240→  const seededContext = testCase.context ?? [];
   241→  const contextMessageIds = seededContext.length > 0
   242→    ? await seedConversationContext(runtime.surreal, conversationRecord, seededContext)
   243→    : [];
   244→
   245→  if (testCase.workspace_seed && testCase.workspace_seed.length > 0) {
   246→    await seedGraphEntities(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, conversationRecord, testCase.workspace_seed);
   247→  }
   248→
   249→  const userMessageRecord = await seedUserMessage(runtime.surreal, conversationRecord, testCase.input);
   250→
   251→  const extractionConversationContext = await loadExtractionConversationContext({
   252→    surreal: runtime.surreal,
   253→    conversationId,
   254→    currentMessageRecord: userMessageRecord,
   255→  });
   256→  const extractionGraphContext = await loadConversationGraphContext(runtime.surreal, conversationId, 60);
   257→  const workspaceProjects = await loadWorkspaceProjects(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord);
   258→  const workspaceProjectNames = workspaceProjects.map((project) => project.name);
   259→
   260→  const extraction = await extractStructuredGraph({
   261→    extractionModel: runtime.extractionModel,
   262→    conversationHistory: extractionConversationContext.conversationHistory,
   263→    currentMessage: extractionConversationContext.currentMessage,
   264→    graphContext: extractionGraphContext,
   265→    sourceText: testCase.input,
   266→    onboarding: true,
   267→    workspaceName,
   268→    projectNames: workspaceProjectNames,
   269→  });
   270→
   271→  const now = new Date();
   272→  const persistence = await persistExtractionOutput({
   273→    surreal: runtime.surreal,
   274→    embeddingModel: runtime.embeddingModel,
   275→    embeddingDimension: runtime.config.embeddingDimension,
   276→    extractionModelId: runtime.config.extractionModelId,
   277→    extractionStoreThreshold: runtime.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   278→    workspaceRecord,
   279→    sourceRecord: userMessageRecord as SourceRecord,
   280→    sourceKind: "message",
   281→    sourceLabel: testCase.input.slice(0, 140),
   282→    promptText: testCase.input,
   283→    output: extraction,
   284→    sourceMessageRecord: userMessageRecord,
   285→    extractionHistoryMessageIds: extractionConversationContext.conversationHistory.map(
   286→      (row) => row.id.id as string,
   287→    ),
   288→    now,
   289→  });
   290→
   291→  await appendExtractedTools(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord, persistence.tools, now);
   292→
   293→  const [evidenceRows] = await runtime.surreal
   294→    .query<[Array<{
   295→      evidence?: string;
   296→      from_text?: string;
   297→      model?: string;
   298→      evidence_source?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   299→      resolved_from?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   300→    }>]>(
   301→      "SELECT evidence, from_text, model, evidence_source, resolved_from FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` = $source;",
   302→      { source: userMessageRecord },
   303→    )
   304→    .collect<[Array<{
   305→      evidence?: string;
   306→      from_text?: string;
   307→      model?: string;
   308→      evidence_source?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   309→      resolved_from?: RecordId<"message", string>;
   310→    }>]>();
   311→
   312→  const personCount = await loadWorkspacePeopleCount(runtime.surreal, workspaceRecord);
   313→  const [workspaceToolRows] = await runtime.surreal
   314→    .query<[Array<{ tools?: string[] }>]>("SELECT tools FROM $workspace LIMIT 1;", {
   315→      workspace: workspaceRecord,
   316→    })
   317→    .collect<[Array<{ tools?: string[] }>]>();
   318→  const extractedTools = workspaceToolRows[0]?.tools ?? [];
   319→
   320→  const [relationRows] = await runtime.surreal
   321→    .query<[Array<{
   322→      kind: string;
   323→      in: { tb: string };
   324→      out: { tb: string };
   325→      from_text: string;
   326→      to_text: string;
   327→      confidence: number;
   328→    }>]>(
   329→      "SELECT kind, `in`, out, from_text, to_text, confidence FROM entity_relation WHERE source_message = $source AND kind != 'POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE';",
   330→      { source: userMessageRecord },
   331→    )
   332→    .collect<[Array<{
   333→      kind: string;
   334→      in: { tb: string };
   335→      out: { tb: string };
   336→      from_text: string;
   337→      to_text: string;
   338→      confidence: number;
   339→    }>]>();
   340→
   341→  const output: ExtractionEvalOutput = {
   342→    caseId: testCase.id,
   343→    input: testCase.input,
   344→    userMessageId: userMessageRecord.id as string,
   345→    contextMessageIds,
   346→    extractedEntities: persistence.entities.map((e) => ({
   347→      kind: e.kind,
   348→      text: e.text,
   349→      confidence: e.confidence,
   350→    })),
   351→    extractedTools,
   352→    personCount,
   353→    ownerPersonCount,
   354→    evidenceRows: evidenceRows.map((row) => ({
   355→      evidence: row.evidence,
   356→      fromText: row.from_text,
   357→      model: row.model,
   358→      evidenceSourceId: row.evidence_source?.id as string | undefined,
   359→      resolvedFromId: row.resolved_from?.id as string | undefined,
   360→    })),
   361→    extractedRelations: relationRows.map((row) => ({
   362→      kind: row.kind,
   363→      fromKind: row.in.tb,
   364→      fromText: row.from_text,
   365→      toKind: row.out.tb,
   366→      toText: row.to_text,
   367→      confidence: row.confidence,
   368→    })),
   369→  };
   370→
   371→  resultCache[cacheKey] = output;
   372→  saveCache(cachePath, resultCache);
   373→  return output;
   374→}
   375→
   376→function loadCache(path: string): Record<string, ExtractionEvalOutput> {
   377→  if (!existsSync(path)) {
   378→    return {};
   379→  }
   380→
   381→  return JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8")) as Record<string, ExtractionEvalOutput>;
   382→}
   383→
   384→function saveCache(path: string, cache: Record<string, ExtractionEvalOutput>): void {
   385→  mkdirSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true });
   386→  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(cache, null, 2));
   387→}
   388→
   389→function assertAutoevalEnv(): void {
   390→  const hasOpenAiKey = hasEnv("OPENAI_API_KEY");
   391→  const hasBraintrustKey = hasEnv("BRAINTRUST_API_KEY");
   392→  if (!hasOpenAiKey && !hasBraintrustKey) {
   393→    throw new Error(
   394→      "Missing evaluator credentials. Set OPENAI_API_KEY (recommended for OPENAI_BASE_URL/OpenRouter) or BRAINTRUST_API_KEY.",
   395→    );
   396→  }
   397→
   398→  if (!hasEnv("OPENAI_BASE_URL")) {
   399→    throw new Error("Missing OPENAI_BASE_URL for autoevals provider routing.");
   400→  }
   401→}
   402→
   403→function requireEnv(name: string): string {
   404→  const value = process.env[name];
   405→  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) {
   406→    throw new Error(`Missing required environment variable: ${name}`);
   407→  }
   408→  return value;
   409→}
   410→
   411→function hasEnv(name: string): boolean {
   412→  const value = process.env[name];
   413→  return value !== undefined && value.trim().length > 0;
   414→}
   415→
   416→function buildCaseCacheKey(modelId: string, testCase: GoldenCase): string {
   417→  const cacheVersion = "classification-v3";
   418→  const caseHash = createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(testCase)).digest("hex").slice(0, 24);
   419→  return `${cacheVersion}:${modelId}:${testCase.id}:${caseHash}`;
   420→}
   421→

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: LLM-Classified Category Field on Task, Question, and Decision Entities (#42)

## Context

Tasks, questions, and decisions have no domain classification. This prevents agents from filtering by area, the Management Agent from reporting by domain, and the governance feed from grouping items. This change adds a `category` field (one of: engineering, research, marketing, operations, design, sales) to these three entity types, classified by the LLM during extraction.

## Scope

In-scope: schema, shared constant, extraction prompt + schema, persistence, CategoryBadge UI, entity detail display, eval golden cases.
Out-of-scope: Management Agent category-aware queries (no Management Agent exists yet), governance feed grouping (issue #30).

---

## Step 1: Shared Category Constant

**File:** `app/src/shared/contracts.ts`

- Add `TaskCategory` type and `ENTITY_CATEGORIES` constant:
  ```typescript
  export const ENTITY_CATEGORIES = ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] as const;
  export type EntityCategory = (typeof ENTITY_CATEGORIES)[number];
  ```
- Add `category?: EntityCategory` to `ExtractedEntity` type (line 36)

## Step 2: SurrealDB Schema

**File:** `schema/surreal-schema.surql`

Add after existing fields on each table:

```sql
-- task table (after line ~93, before indexes)
DEFINE FIELD category ON task TYPE option<string>
  ASSERT $value IN ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] OR $value IS NONE;

-- decision table (after line ~114, before indexes)
DEFINE FIELD category ON decision TYPE option<string>
  ASSERT $value IN ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] OR $value IS NONE;

-- question table (after line ~130, before indexes)
DEFINE FIELD category ON question TYPE option<string>
  ASSERT $value IN ["engineering", "research", "marketing", "operations", "design", "sales"] OR $value IS NONE;
```

## Step 3: Extraction Zod Schema

**File:** `app/src/server/extraction/schema.ts`

- Import `ENTITY_CATEGORIES` from `../../shared/contracts`
- Add `category` as optional field to `extractionEntityBaseSchema`:
  ```typescript
  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),
  ```
- The discriminated union variants (with assignee, resolvedFrom, both) inherit from base, so they all get `category` automatically

## Step 4: Extraction Prompt

**File:** `app/src/server/extraction/prompt.ts`

- Import `ENTITY_CATEGORIES` from `../../shared/contracts`
- Add category classification instructions after the kind classification rules block (~line 37):
  ```
  Category classification rules:
  - For task, decision, and question entities, classify category based on the area the entity affects.
  - Categories: engineering, research, marketing, operations, design, sales.
  - engineering: building, implementing, fixing, deploying code.
  - research: investigating, evaluating, comparing, exploring unknowns.
  - marketing: outreach, content, positioning, launch activities.
  - operations: setup, configuration, process, infrastructure admin.
  - design: wireframes, prototypes, UX, visual design, user flows.
  - sales: prospect outreach, demos, pitching, qualifying leads.
  - Classify by what the entity affects, not where it came up. "Use JWT" is engineering even if discussed during design.
  - Do not set category on project or feature entities.
  ```
- Update the output schema rules section to mention category is optional for task/decision/question

## Step 5: Entity Persistence

**File:** `app/src/server/extraction/entity-upsert.ts`

- Update `buildEntityRecordContent()` (line 254) to accept and pass `category`:
  - Add `category?: string` parameter
  - For task (line 282): spread `...(category ? { category } : {})`
  - For decision (line 295): spread `...(category ? { category } : {})`
  - For question (line 308): spread `...(category ? { category } : {})`
- Update the call site at line 119 to pass `input.extracted.category`:
  ```typescript
  buildEntityRecordContent(
    input.extracted.kind,
    input.extracted.text,
    input.extracted.confidence,
    input.now,
    candidateEmbedding,
    input.sourceMessageRecord,
    "category" in input.extracted ? input.extracted.category : undefined,
  ),
  ```

**File:** `app/src/server/extraction/persist-extraction.ts`

- Update `persistedEntities.push()` (line 111) to include category:
  ```typescript
  ...("category" in extracted ? { category: extracted.category } : {}),
  ```
- Same for `seeds.push()` (line 120)

**File:** `app/src/shared/contracts.ts`

- Add `category?: EntityCategory` to `ExtractedEntity` (line 36)
- Add `category?: EntityCategory` to `OnboardingSeedItem` (line 58)

## Step 6: CategoryBadge Component

**File:** `app/src/client/components/graph/CategoryBadge.tsx` (new)

```typescript
import type { EntityCategory } from "../../../shared/contracts";

const CATEGORY_CONFIG: Record<EntityCategory, { icon: string; label: string }> = {
  engineering: { icon: "\u2699\uFE0F", label: "Engineering" },
  research: { icon: "\uD83D\uDD0D", label: "Research" },
  marketing: { icon: "\uD83D\uDCE2", label: "Marketing" },
  operations: { icon: "\uD83D\uDD27", label: "Operations" },
  design: { icon: "\uD83C\uDFA8", label: "Design" },
  sales: { icon: "\uD83E\uDD1D", label: "Sales" },
};

export function CategoryBadge({ category }: { category: EntityCategory }) {
  const config = CATEGORY_CONFIG[category];
  return (
    <span className="category-badge">
      {config.icon} {config.label}
    </span>
  );
}
```

**File:** `app/src/client/styles.css`

Add CSS for `.category-badge` — small pill, muted neutral background, similar style to `.entity-badge-inline` but smaller/subtler.

**File:** `app/src/client/chat-component-catalog.tsx`

- In the `EntityCard` component, render CategoryBadge below the kind label when `props.category` exists

**File:** `evals/data/golden-cases.json`

- Add `expectedCategory` to existing task/decision cases where classification is unambiguous (e.g., "decision-basic" → engineering)
- Add new category-focused golden cases from the issue's eval section:
  - `category-task-research`: "We need to research pricing models" → task, research
  - `category-task-engineering`: "Let's build the auth middleware next" → task, engineering
  - `category-task-sales`: "I should reach out to potential customers" → task, sales
  - `category-task-operations`: "Need to set up CI/CD pipeline" → task, operations
  - `category-task-design`: "Design the dashboard wireframes" → task, design
  - `category-task-marketing`: "Write a launch blog post" → task, marketing
  - `category-decision-engineering`: "We'll use PostgreSQL for the main database" → decision, engineering
  - `category-decision-sales`: "Let's target mid-market B2B companies first" → decision, sales
  - `category-decision-design`: "We'll go with a single-page onboarding flow" → decision, design
  - `category-decision-operations`: "Let's use Terraform for infrastructure" → decision, operations
  - `category-decision-marketing`: "We'll position as a developer-first platform" → decision, marketing

**File:** `evals/scorers/category-accuracy.ts` (new)

- New scorer that checks `expectedCategory` against extracted entity's category
- Score 1.0 if no expectedCategory on the case, score 1.0 if match, 0.0 if mismatch or missing

**File:** `evals/extraction.eval.ts`

- Register the new category-accuracy scorer

- Wire category through from persistence result

Step 3 — Extraction Zod schema. The schemas use `.strict()` which means I need to be careful. Since `category` is optional and added to the base, the extended variants that use `.extend()` and `.strict()` will need it too. Let me look at how the union works — actually since `extractionEntityBaseSchema` has `.strict()` and the variants extend from it and also call `.strict()`, I need to add `category` to the base schema before `.strict()` is called.

Wait, looking at the code again: the base schema is defined with `.strict()` on line 9. The variants extend from `extractionEntityBaseSchema` and also call `.strict()`. Since `.extend()` on a strict schema produces a new schema that includes the extended fields, and then `.strict()` is re-applied, the category field added to the base will be inherited by all variants.

Actually, let me re-read: `extractionEntityBaseSchema` is the object with `.strict()`. When I add `category` to it, all extend variants will inherit it. But wait — the variants use `extractionEntityBaseSchema.extend({...}).strict()`. The `.extend()` call on a strict schema creates a new schema that includes all base fields plus extensions. The category field added to the base will be included.

     1→import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
     2→import { useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
     3→import type { EntityDetailResponse, EntityKind } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { EntityBadge } from "./EntityBadge";
     5→import { RelationshipList } from "./RelationshipList";
     6→import { ProvenanceSection } from "./ProvenanceSection";
     7→import { useViewState } from "../../stores/view-state";
     8→import { confirmDecision, overrideDecision, markTaskComplete } from "../../graph/actions";
     9→
    10→const CONFIRMABLE_STATUSES = new Set(["extracted", "proposed", "provisional", "inferred"]);
    11→
    12→export function EntityDetailPanel({
    13→  entityId,
    14→  workspaceId,
    15→  onClose,
    16→  onEntityClick,
    17→}: {
    18→  entityId: string;
    19→  workspaceId: string;
    20→  onClose: () => void;
    21→  onEntityClick: (entityId: string) => void;
    22→}) {
    23→  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<EntityDetailResponse | undefined>();
    24→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    25→  const [error, setError] = useState<string | undefined>();
    26→  const [actionPending, setActionPending] = useState(false);
    27→  const navigateToChat = useViewState((s) => s.navigateToChat);
    28→  const navigate = useNavigate();
    29→
    30→  useEffect(() => {
    31→    setLoading(true);
    32→    setError(undefined);
    33→
    34→    fetch(`/api/entities/${encodeURIComponent(entityId)}?workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(workspaceId)}`)
    35→      .then(async (response) => {
    36→        if (!response.ok) {
    37→          const body = await response.text();
    38→          throw new Error(body);
    39→        }
    40→        return response.json() as Promise<EntityDetailResponse>;
    41→      })
    42→      .then((data) => {
    43→        setDetail(data);
    44→        setLoading(false);
    45→      })
    46→      .catch((err) => {
    47→        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to load entity");
    48→        setLoading(false);
    49→      });
    50→  }, [entityId, workspaceId]);
    51→
    52→  function handleJumpToMessage(messageId: string) {
    53→    navigateToChat(messageId);
    54→    void navigate({ to: "/" });
    55→  }
    56→
    57→  async function handleConfirm() {
    58→    if (!detail || actionPending) return;
    59→    setActionPending(true);
    60→    try {
    61→      await confirmDecision(workspaceId, entityId);
    62→      setDetail((prev) =>
    63→        prev
    64→          ? { ...prev, entity: { ...prev.entity, data: { ...prev.entity.data, status: "confirmed" } } }
    65→          : prev,
    66→      );
    67→    } finally {
    68→      setActionPending(false);
    69→    }
    70→  }
    71→
    72→  async function handleOverride() {
    73→    if (!detail || actionPending) return;
    74→    const newSummary = window.prompt("New decision summary:");
    75→    if (!newSummary) return;
    76→    setActionPending(true);
    77→    try {
    78→      await overrideDecision(workspaceId, entityId, newSummary);
    79→      setDetail((prev) =>
    80→        prev
    81→          ? {
    82→              ...prev,
    83→              entity: {
    84→                ...prev.entity,
    85→                name: newSummary,
    86→                data: { ...prev.entity.data, status: "overridden", summary: newSummary },
    87→              },
    88→            }
    89→          : prev,
    90→      );
    91→    } finally {
    92→      setActionPending(false);
    93→    }
    94→  }
    95→
    96→  async function handleComplete() {
    97→    if (!detail || actionPending) return;
    98→    setActionPending(true);
    99→    try {
   100→      await markTaskComplete(workspaceId, entityId);
   101→      setDetail((prev) =>
   102→        prev
   103→          ? { ...prev, entity: { ...prev.entity, data: { ...prev.entity.data, status: "done" } } }
   104→          : prev,
   105→      );
   106→    } finally {
   107→      setActionPending(false);
   108→    }
   109→  }
   110→
   111→  if (loading) {
   112→    return (
   113→      <aside className="entity-detail-panel">
   114→        <p className="entity-detail-meta">Loading...</p>
   115→      </aside>
   116→    );
   117→  }
   118→
   119→  if (error || !detail) {
   120→    return (
   121→      <aside className="entity-detail-panel">
   122→        <div className="entity-detail-header">
   123→          <p className="entity-detail-meta">{error ?? "Entity not found"}</p>
   124→          <button type="button" className="entity-detail-close" onClick={onClose}>&times;</button>
   125→        </div>
   126→      </aside>
   127→    );
   128→  }
   129→
   130→  const kind = detail.entity.kind as EntityKind;
   131→  const status = (detail.entity.data.status as string | undefined) ?? "";
   132→  const rationale = detail.entity.data.rationale as string | undefined;
   133→
   134→  const showConfirm = kind === "decision" && CONFIRMABLE_STATUSES.has(status);
   135→  const showOverride = kind === "decision" && CONFIRMABLE_STATUSES.has(status);
   136→  const showComplete = kind === "task" && status !== "done";
   137→
   138→  return (
   139→    <aside className="entity-detail-panel">
   140→      <div className="entity-detail-header">
   141→        <div>
   142→          <EntityBadge kind={kind} />
   143→          <h3>{detail.entity.name}</h3>
   144→        </div>
   145→        <button type="button" className="entity-detail-close" onClick={onClose}>&times;</button>
   146→      </div>
   147→
   148→      <div className="entity-detail-section">
   149→        <h4>Metadata</h4>
   150→        <dl className="entity-detail-meta">
   151→          {status ? (
   152→            <>
   153→              <dt>Status</dt>
   154→              <dd>{status}</dd>
   155→            </>
   156→          ) : undefined}
   157→          {detail.entity.data.confidence !== undefined ? (
   158→            <>
   159→              <dt>Confidence</dt>
   160→              <dd>{(detail.entity.data.confidence as number).toFixed(2)}</dd>
   161→            </>
   162→          ) : undefined}
   163→          {detail.entity.data.created_at ? (
   164→            <>
   165→              <dt>Created</dt>
   166→              <dd>{new Date(detail.entity.data.created_at as string).toLocaleDateString()}</dd>
   167→            </>
   168→          ) : undefined}
   169→          {detail.entity.data.owner_name ? (
   170→            <>
   171→              <dt>Owner</dt>
   172→              <dd>{detail.entity.data.owner_name as string}</dd>
   173→            </>
   174→          ) : undefined}
   175→          {detail.entity.data.priority ? (
   176→            <>
   177→              <dt>Priority</dt>
   178→              <dd>{detail.entity.data.priority as string}</dd>
   179→            </>
   180→          ) : undefined}
   181→        </dl>
   182→      </div>
   183→
   184→      {rationale ? (
   185→        <div className="entity-detail-section">
   186→          <h4>Rationale</h4>
   187→          <p className="entity-detail-meta">{rationale}</p>
   188→        </div>
   189→      ) : undefined}
   190→
   191→      <RelationshipList relationships={detail.relationships} onEntityClick={onEntityClick} />
   192→
   193→      <ProvenanceSection provenance={detail.provenance} onJumpToMessage={handleJumpToMessage} />
   194→
   195→      {(showConfirm || showOverride || showComplete) ? (
   196→        <div className="entity-detail-actions">
   197→          {showConfirm ? (
   198→            <button type="button" disabled={actionPending} onClick={handleConfirm}>
   199→              Confirm
   200→            </button>
   201→          ) : undefined}
   202→          {showOverride ? (
   203→            <button type="button" disabled={actionPending} onClick={handleOverride}>
   204→              Override
   205→            </button>
   206→          ) : undefined}
   207→          {showComplete ? (
   208→            <button type="button" disabled={actionPending} onClick={handleComplete}>
   209→              Mark Complete
   210→            </button>
   211→          ) : undefined}
   212→        </div>
   213→      ) : undefined}
   214→    </aside>
   215→  );
   216→}
   217→
```

    22→      <circle cx="6" cy="6" r="3" />

    27→    </svg>

    29→}

    31→function EntityCard(props: EntityCardProps) {
    32→  const navigateToGraph = useViewState((s) => s.navigateToGraph);
    33→
    34→  function handleGraphClick() {
    35→    if (!props.entityId) return;
    36→    navigateToGraph(props.entityId);
    37→    window.location.pathname = "/graph";
    38→  }
    39→
    40→  return (
    41→    <article className="entity-card">
    42→      <div className="entity-card-header">

    44→        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "0.3rem" }}>
    45→          {props.entityId ? (
    46→            <button
    47→              type="button"
    48→              className="entity-card-graph-link"
    49→              title="View in graph"
    50→              onClick={handleGraphClick}
    51→            >
    52→              <GraphLinkIcon />
    53→            </button>
    54→          ) : undefined}
    55→          <span className="entity-status">{props.status}</span>
    56→        </div>
    57→      </div>
    58→      <p className="entity-name">{props.name}</p>
    59→      <p className="entity-confidence">Confidence {props.confidence.toFixed(2)}</p>
    60→    </article>
    61→  );
    62→}
    63→
    64→function ExtractionSummary(props: ExtractionSummaryProps) {

    69→        {props.entities.map((entity) => (
    70→          <EntityCard
    71→            key={`${entity.kind}:${entity.name.toLowerCase()}`}
    72→            kind={entity.kind}
    73→            name={entity.name}
    74→            confidence={entity.confidence}
    75→            status={entity.status}
    76→            entityId={entity.entityId}
    77→          />
    78→        ))}
    79→      </div>
    80→      <p className="extraction-summary-meta">{props.relationshipCount} high-confidence relationships detected</p>
    81→    </section>
    82→  );
    83→}
    84→
    85→export const chatComponentCatalog = componentCatalog({
    86→  EntityCard: {

    88→    component: EntityCard as any,
    89→  },

    92→    component: ExtractionSummary as any,

```
     1→import type { EntityKind, ExtractedEntity, ExtractedRelationship } from "../../shared/contracts";
     2→import { normalizeName } from "./normalize";
     3→import { shouldDisplayExtraction } from "./validation";
     4→
     5→type ExtractableEntityKind = Exclude<EntityKind, "workspace">;
     6→
     7→type CardEntity = {
     8→  kind: ExtractableEntityKind;
     9→  name: string;
    10→  confidence: number;
    11→  status: "captured";
    12→};
    13→
    14→export function buildExtractionComponentBlock(
    15→  entities: ExtractedEntity[],
    16→  relationships: ExtractedRelationship[],
    17→  displayThreshold: number,
    18→): string | undefined {
    19→  const summaryEntities = new Map<string, CardEntity>();
    20→
    21→  for (const entity of [...entities].sort((a, b) => b.confidence - a.confidence)) {
    22→    if (entity.kind === "workspace" || !shouldDisplayExtraction(entity.confidence, displayThreshold)) {
    23→      continue;
    24→    }
    25→
    26→    const name = entity.text.trim();
    27→    if (name.length === 0) {
    28→      continue;
    29→    }
    30→
    31→    const key = `${entity.kind}:${normalizeName(name)}`;
    32→    if (!summaryEntities.has(key)) {
    33→      summaryEntities.set(key, {
    34→        kind: entity.kind as ExtractableEntityKind,
    35→        name,
    36→        confidence: entity.confidence,
    37→        status: "captured",
    38→      });
    39→    }
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const cards = [...summaryEntities.values()].slice(0, 6);
    43→  if (cards.length === 0) {
    44→    return undefined;
    45→  }
    46→
    47→  const componentSpec = cards.length === 1
    48→    ? {
    49→        type: "EntityCard",
    50→        props: cards[0],
    51→      }
    52→    : {
    53→        type: "ExtractionSummary",
    54→        props: {
    55→          title: "Captured from your latest message",
    56→          entities: cards,
    57→          relationshipCount: relationships.filter((relationship) =>
    58→            shouldDisplayExtraction(relationship.confidence, displayThreshold)
    59→          ).length,
    60→        },
    61→      };
    62→
    63→  return ["```component", JSON.stringify(componentSpec, null, 2), "```"].join("\n");
    64→}
    65→
```

     1→import { createScorer } from "evalite";
     2→import type { GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput } from "../types";
     3→import { matchesExpectedEntity } from "./shared";
     4→
     5→export const entityRecallScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({
     6→  name: "entity-recall",
     7→  description: "Percent of expected entities present in extraction output by kind and text/text_contains expectation.",
     8→  scorer: ({ output, expected }) => {
     9→    const expectedEntities = expected?.expectedEntities ?? [];
    10→    if (expectedEntities.length === 0) {
    11→      return { score: 1 };
    12→    }
    13→
    14→    let matched = 0;
    15→    for (const expectedEntity of expectedEntities) {
    16→      const found = output.extractedEntities.some((entity) => matchesExpectedEntity(expectedEntity, entity, 0.5));
    17→      if (found) {
    18→        matched += 1;
    19→      }
    20→    }
    21→
    22→    return { score: matched / expectedEntities.length };
    23→  },
    24→});
    25→

```
     1→import type { ExpectedEntity } from "../types";
     2→
     3→function normalize(value: string): string {
     4→  return value
     5→    .toLowerCase()
     6→    .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
     7→    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
     8→    .trim();
     9→}
    10→
    11→function tokenize(value: string): string[] {
    12→  return normalize(value)
    13→    .split(" ")
    14→    .filter((token) => token.length > 0);
    15→}
    16→
    17→export function nameSimilarity(a: string, b: string): number {
    18→  const normalizedA = normalize(a);
    19→  const normalizedB = normalize(b);
    20→  const aTokens = new Set(tokenize(normalizedA));
    21→  const bTokens = new Set(tokenize(normalizedB));
    22→
    23→  if (normalizedA.length === 0 || normalizedB.length === 0 || aTokens.size === 0 || bTokens.size === 0) {
    24→    return 0;
    25→  }
    26→
    27→  const overlap = [...aTokens].filter((token) => bTokens.has(token)).length;
    28→  const minSize = Math.min(aTokens.size, bTokens.size);
    29→  const union = new Set([...aTokens, ...bTokens]).size;
    30→  const jaccard = overlap / union;
    31→  const overlapRatio = overlap / minSize;
    32→  const substringBonus = normalizedA.includes(normalizedB) || normalizedB.includes(normalizedA) ? 0.2 : 0;
    33→  const exactBonus = normalizedA === normalizedB ? 0.2 : 0;
    34→
    35→  return Math.min(1, Math.max(jaccard, overlapRatio * 0.85) + substringBonus + exactBonus);
    36→}
    37→
    38→export function isEntityNameMatch(a: string, b: string, threshold = 0.5): boolean {
    39→  return nameSimilarity(a, b) >= threshold;
    40→}
    41→
    42→export function normalizeForSubstring(value: string): string {
    43→  return normalize(value);
    44→}
    45→
    46→export function matchesExpectedEntity(
    47→  expectedEntity: ExpectedEntity,
    48→  extractedEntity: { kind: string; text: string },
    49→  threshold = 0.5,
    50→): boolean {
    51→  if (expectedEntity.kind !== extractedEntity.kind) {
    52→    return false;
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  if ("text_contains" in expectedEntity) {
    56→    const expectedSubstring = normalizeForSubstring(expectedEntity.text_contains);
    57→    if (expectedSubstring.length === 0) {
    58→      return false;
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    return normalizeForSubstring(extractedEntity.text).includes(expectedSubstring);
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  return isEntityNameMatch(expectedEntity.text, extractedEntity.text, threshold);
    65→}
    66→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/palembang/evals/scorers/category-accuracy.ts",
  "content": "import { createScorer } from \"evalite\";\nimport type { GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput } from \"../types\";\nimport { matchesExpectedEntity } from \"./shared\";\n\nexport const categoryAccuracyScorer = createScorer<GoldenCase, ExtractionEvalOutput, GoldenCase>({\n  name: \"category-accuracy\",\n  description: \"Percent of expected entities with expectedCategory whose matched extraction has the correct category.\",\n  scorer: ({ output, expected }) => {\n    const expectedEntities = expected?.expectedEntities ?? [];\n    const withCategory = expectedEntities.filter((e) => e.expectedCategory);\n    if (withCategory.length === 0) {\n      return { score: 1 };\n    }\n\n    let matched = 0;\n    for (const expectedEntity of withCategory) {\n      const found = output.extractedEntities.find((entity) =>\n        matchesExpectedEntity(expectedEntity, entity, 0.5),\n      );\n      if (found && found.category === expectedEntity.expectedCategory) {\n        matched += 1;\n      }\n    }\n\n    return { score: matched / withCategory.length };\n  },\n});\n"
}

Everything looks correct. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Implementation Complete

**16 files changed** across 8 steps for GitHub issue #42:

### Shared Types (`contracts.ts`)
- Added `ENTITY_CATEGORIES` const array and `EntityCategory` type as single source of truth
- Added optional `category` field to `ExtractedEntity` and `OnboardingSeedItem`

### SurrealDB Schema (`surreal-schema.surql`)
- Added `category` field with enum ASSERT constraint to `task`, `decision`, and `question` tables

### Extraction Pipeline
- **Zod schema** (`schema.ts`): Added optional `category` field with `z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES)` to the base entity schema — inherited by all union variants
- **Prompt** (`prompt.ts`): Added category classification rules (6 categories with definitions, classify by area affected, not by conversation context)
- **Persistence** (`entity-upsert.ts`): `buildEntityRecordContent()` now accepts and persists `category` for task/decision/question
- **Event emission** (`persist-extraction.ts`): Category flows through to `persistedEntities` and `seeds` arrays, reaching SSE events and UI

### UI
- **CategoryBadge** (`CategoryBadge.tsx`): New component with emoji icon + label per category
- **EntityDetailPanel**: Shows CategoryBadge in metadata section when category exists
- **EntityCard** in chat: Shows CategoryBadge next to kind label
- **chat-component-definitions**: Added `category` to `entityCardPropsSchema`
- **components.ts**: Category flows through to extraction summary cards

### Evals
- **Types**: Added `expectedCategory` to `ExpectedEntity`, `category` to eval output
- **Scorer** (`category-accuracy.ts`): Checks expected vs actual category for matched entities
- **Golden cases**: 11 new category-focused cases (6 tasks, 5 decisions covering all categories) + annotated 2 existing cases
- **Weight config**: Added `category-accuracy` at 10% weight, slightly reduced other weights to compensate

All 117 pre-existing unit tests continue to pass.

> AGENT

The error is clear — the OpenRouter/Azure provider requires that when using structured output (`response_format`), every property in the JSON schema must be listed in `required`. The Zod `.optional()` field works for validation but the generated JSON schema sent to the LLM has `category` in `properties` without it being in `required`, which Azure rejects.

The fix: since the Zod schema variants use `.strict()` and are combined in a `z.union()` (which maps to `anyOf`), each variant needs `category` in its `required` array. The issue is that Zod's `.optional()` doesn't add the field to `required` in the JSON schema output, but Azure's structured output demands all properties be required.

The solution is to make `category` required in the schema but allow an explicit empty/null signal. However, per the project conventions we can't use `null`. Let me check how the `ai` package converts the Zod schema.

> AGENT

The Vercel AI SDK's `generateObject` sends the Zod schema as a JSON schema to the provider. Azure's structured output requires every property in `properties` to be in `required` — it doesn't support optional fields. The existing codebase handles this with union variants (e.g., `assignee_name` is required on its variant, not optional on the base).

The cleanest fix: make `category` required in the extraction schema with a `"none"` sentinel, then strip it after parsing. This avoids an explosion of union variants.

> TOOL

     2→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→
     4→const extractionEntityBaseSchema = z.object({
     5→  tempId: z.string().min(1),
     6→  kind: z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"]),
     7→  text: z.string().min(3).max(200),
     8→  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1),
     9→  evidence: z.string().min(1),
    10→  category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional(),
    11→}).strict();
    12→
    13→const extractionEntityWithAssigneeSchema = extractionEntityBaseSchema.extend({
    14→  assignee_name: z.string().min(1),
    15→}).strict();
    16→
    17→const extractionEntityWithResolvedFromSchema = extractionEntityBaseSchema.extend({
    18→  resolvedFromMessageId: z.string().min(1),
    19→}).strict();
    20→
    21→const extractionEntityWithAssigneeAndResolvedFromSchema = extractionEntityBaseSchema.extend({
    22→  assignee_name: z.string().min(1),
    23→  resolvedFromMessageId: z.string().min(1),
    24→}).strict();
    25→
    26→const extractionEntitySchema = z.union([
    27→  extractionEntityBaseSchema,
    28→  extractionEntityWithAssigneeSchema,
    29→  extractionEntityWithResolvedFromSchema,
    30→  extractionEntityWithAssigneeAndResolvedFromSchema,
    31→]);
    32→
    33→export const extractionRelationshipSchema = z.object({
    34→  kind: z.string().min(1),
    35→  fromTempId: z.string().min(1),
    36→  toTempId: z.string().min(1),
    37→  confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1),
    38→  fromText: z.string().min(1),
    39→  toText: z.string().min(1),
    40→}).strict();
    41→
    42→export const extractionResultSchema = z.object({
    43→  entities: z.array(extractionEntitySchema),
    44→  relationships: z.array(extractionRelationshipSchema),
    45→  tools: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    46→}).strict();
    47→
    48→export type ExtractionPromptEntity = z.infer<typeof extractionEntitySchema>;
    49→export type ExtractionPromptRelationship = z.infer<typeof extractionRelationshipSchema>;
    50→export type ExtractionPromptOutput = z.infer<typeof extractionResultSchema>;
    51→
    52→export function parseExtractionOutput(payload: unknown):
    53→  | { ok: true; data: ExtractionPromptOutput }
    54→  | { ok: false; error: string } {
    55→  const result = extractionResultSchema.safeParse(payload);
    56→  if (result.success) {
    57→    return { ok: true, data: result.data };
    58→  }
    59→
    60→  return {
    61→    ok: false,
    62→    error: result.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "Invalid extraction payload",
    63→  };
    64→}

> AGENT

Fixed. The issue was Azure's structured output requiring every property in `properties` to also be in `required`. The Zod `.optional()` field was excluded from `required` in the generated JSON schema, causing the provider to reject it.

**Fix**: Changed `category` from `z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional()` to a required `z.enum([...ENTITY_CATEGORIES, "none"])` with a `.transform()` that converts `"none"` to `undefined` after parsing. The prompt now instructs the LLM to use `"none"` for project/feature entities. All 125 unit tests pass.

> DEVELOPER
